# WPF Gallery Milestone 1 Tracker

Last updated: 2026-05-24

## Goal

Make ModernWpf Gallery visually near-identical to the official WPF Gallery for
all WPF Gallery-equivalent pages and controls, while keeping ModernWpf and
WinUI-style controls such as `NavigationView` where they fit the WinUI Gallery
interaction model.

## Source References

| Source | Local path | Use |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Official WPF Gallery | `D:\repos\WPF-Samples\Sample Applications\WPFGallery` | Primary visual, page, copy, sample, and catalog reference. |
| Official WinUI Gallery | `D:\repos\WinUI-Gallery` | Primary source and visual reference for ModernWpf/WinUI extension pages and WinUI control pages implemented in WPF. |
| ModernWpf Gallery | `ModernWpf.Gallery` | Target implementation. |
| Gallery runtime tests | `test\ModernWpf.Gallery.Tests` | Main regression layer for route, page, shell, catalog, and sample parity checks. |

Use the local checkouts above as the source of truth. Do not web-search WinUI
Gallery source while `D:\repos\WinUI-Gallery` is available.

## Copy vs Adapt Rule

Prefer copying official WPF Gallery page structure, sample XAML, page titles,
descriptions, images, spacing, and labels when the target page is a direct WPF
Gallery equivalent.

Adapt instead of raw-copying when the source depends on:

- `WPFGallery` namespaces, navigation services, or view-model plumbing that does
  not exist in ModernWpf Gallery.
- Official WPF Gallery resource keys, converters, or helpers that need to map
  to ModernWpf resource names or local helper controls.
- ModernWpf-specific routing, catalog aliases, test hooks, or automation IDs.
- Pages where ModernWpf intentionally keeps a distinct WinUI/ModernWpf control
  page next to a WPF Gallery page, such as `Calendar` vs `CalendarView` and
  `RichTextEdit` vs `RichEditBox`.
- Controls or interactions that WPF Gallery implements with app-local helpers
  but ModernWpf already has an equivalent reusable control.

In short: the official WPF Gallery is the source of truth for WPF-equivalent
visuals, but most files still need namespace, resource, route, and test
adaptation before they can live in this repo.

## Architecture Direction

The current broad parity pass got WPF Gallery-equivalent pages represented, but
it still relies too much on ModernWpf-specific sample factories that recreate
official WPF Gallery pages in C#. That makes visual parity slower and easier to
drift.

The preferred next architecture is closer to WPF Gallery for WPF-equivalent
pages:

- Keep the ModernWpf outer shell, catalog, and `NavigationView` model so the
  gallery can still host ModernWpf/WinUI-specific pages.
- Add or strengthen a WPF Gallery compatibility layer inside that shell:
  WPF-style page view models with `PageTitle`, `PageDescription`, and sample
  code strings; WPF Gallery-like `PageHeader`, `ControlExample`, `HeaderTile`,
  and navigation-card components; and explicit catalog item to page mappings.
- Prefer adapted `.xaml` pages copied from official WPF Gallery for direct
  WPF-equivalent pages instead of rebuilding those pages through C# sample
  factories.
- Keep C# sample factories for ModernWpf/WinUI-specific pages, generated sample
  content, or pages that do not exist in official WPF Gallery.
- Treat the official WPF Gallery view model/resource/navigation shape as the
  default design for WPF pages, then adapt only the integration points required
  by ModernWpf routing, resource naming, tests, and mixed WPF/WinUI catalog
  support.

This should make future parity rounds more mechanical: copy official page XAML
and view-model state, adapt namespaces/resources/routes, add targeted tests, and
record the checklist status below.

Current architecture status: top-level WPF Gallery-equivalent pages now keep
their default ModernWpf routing constructors while also exposing official-style
constructor-injected view-model paths for Home, All Controls, What's New,
Settings, and WPF-equivalent section pages. The retained ModernWpf shell now
routes WPF Gallery section groups through official-named section page subclasses
while keeping generic `SectionPage` only for ModernWpf extension groups. Home,
All Controls, What's New, and section page constructors now follow the official
WPF Gallery `Views` order of `InitializeComponent()`, then `ViewModel`, then
`DataContext`; Settings keeps its matching official Settings order. The copied
WPF item pages that use this same official init-first constructor shape now
match it as well: Colors, Icons, Typography, Spacing, Geometry, and User
Dashboard. This keeps shell navigation unchanged while moving page construction
closer to the official WPF Gallery `Views` pattern.

## Commit Policy

Commit after each coherent milestone round, but avoid small commits. Fold tiny
documentation-only notes, isolated cleanup, and minor tracker wording tweaks into
the next substantive alignment or verification commit unless the user explicitly
asks for a standalone commit.

## Done

| Area | Status | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Official WPF Gallery source checkout | Done | Cloned under `D:\repos\WPF-Samples`. |
| WPF Gallery catalog sections | Done | WPF Gallery-equivalent sections are represented, including Design Guidance, Samples, Basic Input, Collections, Date & Calendar, Layout, Media, Navigation, Status & Info, Text, and System. The visible navigation order now keeps the official WPF section sequence contiguous through System, then places the orphaned Media section and ModernWpf/WinUI extension groups after the official block. |
| ModernWpf/WinUI page preservation | Done | Distinct ModernWpf pages are kept where aliases overlap WPF pages. |
| Page shell and header model | Mostly done | Generic non-direct `ItemPage` wrapper headers, direct-page wrapper header collapse, section, support, sample chrome, Home header tile strip, Home hero/section heading declarations, navigation-card resources, category/all-controls headers, What's New, Settings, WPF Gallery-style navigation page binding paths, WPF Gallery-style top-level `Page` roots for Home/section/All Controls/What's New/Settings, official-named WPF section page subclasses, official WPF section `Page.Title` values, hidden shell `Frame` content hosting, WPF Gallery-style 44px app title row, WPF Gallery-style `ViewModel.ApplicationTitle` binding for both the window title and visible title text, WPF Gallery-style Back button `ViewModel.BackCommand` / `ViewModel.CanNavigateback` bindings, WPF Gallery-style Settings footer command routed through `MainWindowViewModel.SettingsCommand` with the existing `BindingProxy` bridging detached retained `NavigationView.PaneFooter` content, official `WindowBackground` shell background resource, official code-created `WindowChrome` setup, official high-contrast/Windows 11 `WindowChrome.NonClientFrameEdges` updates, hidden NavigationView pane toggle/header/search chrome, official `Navigation Pane` automation naming, NavigationView item glyph/text columns, WPF Gallery-style top-level and child row insets, WPF Gallery-style left disclosure chevrons for section groups, tight title text UIA bounds, 42px row rhythm aligned to the official TreeView, startup-collapsed shell navigation groups, adapted official `SettingsButton` footer/separator geometry and automation notification, neutralized retained `NavigationView` content-grid chrome, adapted content-frame geometry, normal-mode suppression of ModernWpf-only visual-test status hooks, named `ContentRootGrid` diagnostic/artifact roots without non-official top-level root automation IDs, shared compatibility-control source shape without a non-official title `TextBlock` automation ID, and the official-style `Resources/PageStyles.xaml` / `Resources/Templates.xaml` resource layer have been moved toward WPF Gallery structure and spacing. The shared `BaseTextBlockStyle` now inherits foreground like official WPF Gallery instead of forcing `TextFillColorPrimaryBrush` from the style. |

## Next Recommended Round

The direct-reference section baseline is now refreshed for Light and Dark, and
Basic Input, Collections, Date & Calendar, Design Guidance, Layout, Media,
Navigation, Samples, Status & Info, System, and Text item pages now match the
official WPF Gallery `Page` root and view-model constructor shape, with direct
pages hosted in a chrome-hidden `Frame` so copied `Page` roots have valid WPF
parenting. WPF-equivalent section group routes now instantiate official-named
section page subclasses through a retained-shell factory, while ModernWpf
extension groups still fall back to generic `SectionPage`. Next, continue
source-backed structural alignment where copied
pages still diverge from official XAML, or inspect and fix visible drift in the
remaining shell-specific surfaces: remaining High Contrast gaps, any residual NavigationView
styling not covered by the TreeView token aliases, aligned left disclosure chevrons, row insets,
and high-drift item pages. Color Signal Dark is now refreshed at delta `0.1`
after the WPF Fluent brush override, and Color Stroke Light is refreshed at
delta `0.04` after the Gallery-level `ControlElevationBorderBrush` rendering
alignment. Home first-viewport Light/Dark
is now accepted at `0` / `0.05` delta with matching dashboard-pane crops, and
Settings Light/Dark have current matching-crop evidence at delta `0.23` / `1.15`
with visual-test ComboBox state matching the requested theme, so avoid
reworking Home or Settings unless a new visual or accessibility regression appears. A later Settings experiment that removed the `GalleryPageRootStyle` root style was rejected and reverted because `artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-090657-380-82584/report.md` worsened Settings Light from accepted `0.23` to `0.62`, so do not retry that root-style removal without new evidence. Visual-audit run folders now use millisecond timestamps plus PowerShell PID with retry-on-collision, so parallel or near-parallel focused runs no longer share a timestamp-only artifact root; still prefer focused slices for clarity, not broad parallel runs just to save wall time. Navigation's shell/content-frame direct slice is also refreshed at
`artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-033112/report.md` and
`artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-033135/report.md` with delta `0`
and matching `868x758` crops, so do not reopen that specific content-frame
path unless new shell evidence regresses. Basic Input's normal-shell section
smoke is refreshed at `artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-034331/report.md`
and `artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-034356/report.md` with delta
`0` and matching `868x758` crops. Layout is refreshed at
`artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-034931/report.md` and
`artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-035031/report.md` with delta `0`,
and Collections, Date & Calendar, Status & Info, System, and Text are refreshed
at `artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-035102/report.md` and
`artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-035209/report.md` with delta `0`
for every case. Prefer focused slices such as one section or one high-drift item
with `-TimeoutSeconds 20` for future normal-shell checks, because the official
app still starts per case and broad runs are slow. For
non-Home routes, check that Modern's accepted source is a rendered content
artifact such as `ContentRootGridRenderedArtifact`, `ContentPagePaneRenderedArtifact`,
or `GalleryContentHostRenderedArtifact` before treating the delta as current
branch-tip evidence; for Home, accepted evidence should use the
`ModernWpfHomeContentRootPane` / `OfficialHomeContentRootPane` crop pair. A
focused Design Guidance refresh at
`artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-041933/report.md` and
`artifacts/wpf-gallery-visual-audit/20260524-042046/report.md` now accepts
Color, Typography, Spacing, Geometry, and Iconography at near-zero first-viewport
direct-reference deltas after direct pages inherited the official body text
size and the previous copied-page padding compensation was removed. Avoid
reopening that Design Guidance item-page drift unless a new regression appears.

Continue with the next highest-impact visible drift from the checklist, likely
remaining High Contrast gaps, other NavigationView styling not covered
by the TreeView token aliases or first-sample refresh, or other item pages that still lack current
matching-crop Light/Dark evidence. For each round:

1. Build ModernWpf Gallery and restore/build the official WPF Gallery checkout
   if needed.
2. Run `.\tools\visual-checks\Run-WpfGalleryVisualAudit.ps1 -Cases <section-and-pages> -Reference OfficialWpfGallery`.
3. Inspect the report, full screenshots, content crops, and UIA trees for shell,
   spacing, typography, card, header, and sample-layout drift.
4. Fix the highest-impact visible mismatch in that section, preferring adapted
   official WPF Gallery XAML/resources where possible.
5. Record accepted artifact paths/findings in this file and commit a coherent
   section-sized change.
